| MP-FIX-INVITE-POOL-SEARCH-001 | DONE | src/app/api/players/recent/route.ts (T1, commit ff99de0), src/app/api/friends/search/route.ts + docs/CTO_BACKLOG.md (T2, commit 36d48f3) | Fixed two confirmed defects from MP-STAB-AUTH-RESILIENCE-001 audit. D1: /api/players/recent returned only the 20 most recently created profiles — hosts could not see most existing users. T1 fix: replaced .limit(20).order(created_at desc) with a priority-ordered, deduped pool via raw SQL using getDbPool(): tier 1 = players the current user follows (player_follows where follower_id=user.id), tier 2 = players who shared any session with the current user (session_players join, ordered by most recent shared session), tier 3 = newest profiles as fill. Deduped across tiers via DISTINCT ON (pid) preserving priority order. Cap raised from 20 to 100. Response shape { id, display_name, avatar_url } unchanged — LobbySection.tsx needs no changes. D2: /api/friends/search used LIMIT 10 with NO ORDER BY — when >10 users matched, Postgres returned arbitrary heap-order rows. T2 fix: added ORDER BY (exact display_name match first via LOWER()=LOWER(), then prefix match via ILIKE query||'%', then display_name ASC) and raised LIMIT from 10 to 50. Existing WHERE clause and metadata fallbacks preserved. pg_trgm index for leading-wildcard search deferred to a separate migration task (logged as DEFERRED-001 in docs/CTO_BACKLOG.md Section J). tsc: 0 errors. eslint: 0 errors. (2026-07-08) |

| MP-FIX-PRACTICE-MODAL-HOME-FLASH-001 | DONE | src/app/home/page.tsx | Fixed home-page flash after clicking "Start Practice" in the practice settings modal. Root cause: handlePracticeStart called setPracticeModalOpen(false) before router.push('/practice'), so the modal closed synchronously and exposed the bare home page during the client-side navigation window before the /practice loading screen rendered. Fix (one file, one function, one line removed): deleted the setPracticeModalOpen(false) call from handlePracticeStart. The modal now stays mounted as a visual shield during navigation and unmounts automatically when the /practice page renders, so the user transitions directly modal → practice loading screen without seeing the home page. The modal onClose path (setPracticeModalOpen(false) at line 307) is preserved for user-initiated closes (✕ / backdrop / Escape). Single source of truth preserved (practiceModalOpen state, no new state source). tsc --noEmit: exit 0. Pre-commit hooks: KC-007 + 45/45 tests pass. Commit: 88c3915. (2026-07-13) |

| MP-FIX-LOBBY-URL-REDIRECT-AFTER-REGISTER-001 | DONE | src/components/AuthModal.tsx, src/app/auth/callback/route.ts | Reviewed full lobby-URL-share → unregistered-user → redirect-after-register flow. Google OAuth, email signin, and email-signup-without-confirmation all correctly preserve the `next` param through middleware → /login → AuthModal → /auth/callback → lobby URL. Two fixes applied. (1) AuthModal.tsx handleEmailAuth: signUp call (line 112) previously passed no emailRedirectTo, so if Supabase email confirmation is ever enabled the confirmation email link would not carry the `next` param and the user would land on `/` instead of the lobby URL. Added options.emailRedirectTo mirroring the Google OAuth path (line 58): `${window.location.origin}/auth/callback?next=${encodeURIComponent(next)}` where next is read from searchParams with `/home` fallback. Defensive fix — zero behavior change while email confirmation is disabled (signUp still returns a session immediately → onClose → router.replace(next)). (2) auth/callback/route.ts GET: the `next` param was used directly in NextResponse.redirect(new URL(next, request.url)) with no validation — an open redirect vulnerability since `new URL("//evil.com", origin)` resolves to `https://evil.com`. Added validation: `rawNext.startsWith("/") && !rawNext.startsWith("//")` else fall back to `/`. Middleware already validated next for the /login redirect path; this closes the callback gap. tsc --noEmit: exit 0. vitest AuthModal.test.tsx (KC-007): 6/6 pass. 2 pre-existing CSS guard failures (BadgePopup.module.css, committed in c19f418) unrelated. (2026-07-13) |

| B15-FIX-001 | DONE | partykit/server.ts | Consolidated broadcastVersionCounter increment to single site inside broadcastStateUpdate(). Root cause: broadcastStateUpdate() READ broadcastVersionCounter but did not increment it (only loadFromDB and applySnapshotAndBroadcast incremented), so any direct broadcastStateUpdate() call (e.g. READY_NEXT handler) broadcast a snapshot with unchanged version — the client's monotonic stale-rejection guard in competeWebSocket.ts silently dropped it, so the readyNames row never rendered after clicking Next Round. Fix (single-writer consolidation): added `this.broadcastVersionCounter += 1;` as the first statement in broadcastStateUpdate() after the `if (!this.snapshot) return;` guard, and removed the now-redundant increments in loadFromDB() and applySnapshotAndBroadcast(). Now every broadcast regardless of caller gets a strictly-increasing version. grep proof: broadcastVersionCounter appears at exactly 3 lines (decl line 273, increment line 892, read line 911) — single writer. tsc --noEmit: 0 errors in partykit/server.ts (7 pre-existing errors in AuthModal.test.tsx unrelated, baseline=7 post-change=7). npx next lint: exit 0. Live smoke test (two-context Playwright, real browser UI): S4 lobby ready + auto-start PASS, S5 round play to ROUND_COMPLETE PASS, S6 host Next → readyNames row renders in BOTH browsers + host data-ready=true PASS (this is the fix — guest now sees the row), S7 guest Next → both entries in both browsers PASS, S8 zero stale-STATE_UPDATE rejection logs in either console PASS, S9 kick-player redirect to /home PASS (regression check for BUG2). Commit: 5a1b404b74d1a444afb3f6cda0830189fa363150. (2026-07-19) |
